**最后尝试回答：** 带人参观一栋建筑：位置、类型、结构、通行、房间和维护。

### 词群 13.01｜建筑怎样从构想到建成 {#group-13-01}

**串联方式：** 从棚屋与建筑设计进入结构和施工，选混凝土、钢材并关注稳定性；obstruct 是阻碍。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-001">
<summary><h4 id="word-13-001" class="vocabulary-word-title">shed<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-13-001" aria-label="定位 shed">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="13-001" data-mark-label="shed" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="shed：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="shed：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 shed" title="在有道查询 shed">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">13-001</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 简易房，棚；v. 流，洒；摆脱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The architecture course examined the erection of a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shed</strong></a> as a simple wooden structure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">建筑学课程以棚屋作为简单木结构，研究其建造。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Even the erection of a small <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shed</strong></a> requires attention to architecture and structure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">即使建一座小棚屋，也需要注意建筑设计和结构。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shed</strong></a>’s unusual architecture made the erection of its curved structure more difficult.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">棚屋独特的建筑设计使弧形结构更难建造。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a garden shed</span> · 花园棚屋</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=construction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">construction</a></span> · 建造</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=framework&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">framework</a></span> · 框架</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-002">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The shed’s unusual <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=architecture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>architecture</strong></a> made the erection of its curved structure more difficult.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">棚屋独特的建筑设计使弧形结构更难建造。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We plan to build new homes and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=construct&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>construct</strong></a> a path that will not obstruct public access.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们计划建新住房，并修一条不妨碍公众通行的小路。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Builders must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=construct&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>construct</strong></a> the wall carefully so that it does not obstruct the road while they build the extension.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">建筑工人建扩建部分时必须小心砌墙，以免挡住道路。</p></li>

</ol>

</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We plan to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=build&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>build</strong></a> new homes and construct a path that will not obstruct public access.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们计划建新住房，并修一条不妨碍公众通行的小路。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The donation will help us <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=found&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>found</strong></a> a charity and establish a permanent office.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这笔捐款将帮助我们创办慈善机构并设立永久办公室。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Cracks in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=concrete&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>concrete</strong></a> prompted a stability check of the steel frame.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">混凝土裂缝促使人们检查钢框架的稳定性。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 13.02｜选择地点并安顿下来 {#group-13-02}

**串联方式：** 确定地点、场所和地标，再说明位置、居住、迁移与定居。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-013">
<summary><h4 id="word-13-013" class="vocabulary-word-title">site<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-13-013" aria-label="定位 site">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="13-013" data-mark-label="site" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="site：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="site：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=site&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 site" title="在有道查询 site">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">13-013</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.地点</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The venue is situated on a historic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=site&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>site</strong></a> beside a landmark that helps visitors locate it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">场地位于历史遗址上，旁边的地标能帮助访客找到它。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use the landmark to locate the venue, which is situated at the northern end of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=site&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>site</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用地标来找到场地，它位于该地点的北端。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The planners hope to locate a new venue on a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=site&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>site</strong></a> situated near the city’s best-known landmark.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">规划者希望把新场地选在靠近城市最知名地标的位置。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a building site</span> · 建筑工地</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The planners hope to locate a new <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=venue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>venue</strong></a> on a site situated near the city’s best-known landmark.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">规划者希望把新场地选在靠近城市最知名地标的位置。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The venue is situated on a historic site beside a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=landmark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>landmark</strong></a> that helps visitors locate it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">场地位于历史遗址上，旁边的地标能帮助访客找到它。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=landmark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>landmark</strong></a> to locate the venue, which is situated at the northern end of the site.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用地标来找到场地，它位于该地点的北端。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The planners hope to locate a new venue on a site situated near the city’s best-known <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=landmark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>landmark</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">规划者希望把新场地选在靠近城市最知名地标的位置。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=location&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">location</a></span> · 位置</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accessibility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accessibility</a></span> · 交通便利性</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-018">
<summary><h4 id="word-13-018" class="vocabulary-word-title">reside<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-13-018" aria-label="定位 reside">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="13-018" data-mark-label="reside" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reside：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reside：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reside&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reside" title="在有道查询 reside">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">13-018</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.居住</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Families who migrate here often settle near relatives and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reside&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reside</strong></a> in houses that several generations inhabit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">迁到这里的家庭常在亲戚附近定居，住进几代人共同居住的房子。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">People may migrate for work, settle permanently and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reside&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reside</strong></a> in districts that other newcomers also inhabit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">人们可能为工作迁移、永久定居，并住在其他新来者也居住的地区。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Some islanders migrate seasonally, while those who settle on the mainland <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reside&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reside</strong></a> in communities that former neighbours also inhabit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一些岛民季节性迁移，而在大陆定居的人住在昔日邻居也居住的社区。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">reside in a city</span> · 居住在城市</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=settlement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">settlement</a></span> · 聚居地</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=migration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">migration</a></span> · 迁移</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">People may migrate for work, settle permanently and reside in districts that other newcomers also <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inhabit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>inhabit</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">人们可能为工作迁移、永久定居，并住在其他新来者也居住的地区。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Some islanders migrate seasonally, while those who settle on the mainland reside in communities that former neighbours also <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inhabit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>inhabit</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一些岛民季节性迁移，而在大陆定居的人住在昔日邻居也居住的社区。</p></li>

### 词群 13.03｜为不同生活选择住所 {#group-13-03}

**串联方式：** 比较高楼、公寓、大宅、旅舍与小屋，再辨地下室、育儿空间、摇篮和庇护处。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-022">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Their <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dwelling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dwelling</strong></a> is an apartment in a skyscraper, though they usually call it a flat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他们的住所是摩天楼中的一套公寓，不过他们通常用 flat 来称呼。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Walkers can stay in a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hostel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hostel</strong></a>, a mountain lodge, a basic hut or a rented cabin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">徒步者可以住青年旅舍、山间度假屋、简易棚屋或租来的小木屋。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The cabin costs more than the hut, while the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hostel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hostel</strong></a> is cheaper than the lodge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小木屋比棚屋贵，而青年旅舍比度假屋便宜。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After leaving the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hostel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hostel</strong></a>, we passed a lodge and a shepherd’s hut before reaching our cabin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">离开旅舍后，我们经过一间度假屋和一间牧羊人棚屋，才到自己的小木屋。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a youth hostel</span> · 青年旅舍</p>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Walkers can stay in a hostel, a mountain <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lodge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lodge</strong></a>, a basic hut or a rented cabin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">徒步者可以住青年旅舍、山间度假屋、简易棚屋或租来的小木屋。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The cabin costs more than the hut, while the hostel is cheaper than the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lodge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lodge</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小木屋比棚屋贵，而青年旅舍比度假屋便宜。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After leaving the hostel, we passed a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lodge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lodge</strong></a> and a shepherd’s hut before reaching our cabin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">离开旅舍后，我们经过一间度假屋和一间牧羊人棚屋，才到自己的小木屋。</p></li>
</ol>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The agent showed us a villa, a mansion and a cottage with a deep <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cellar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cellar</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">中介带我们看了别墅、豪宅和一间有深地窖的乡间小屋。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The tour visits a royal mansion, a seaside villa and a cottage where food was once stored in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cellar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cellar</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">行程参观王室宅邸、海滨别墅，以及一间曾用地窖储食的乡间小屋。</p></li>
</ol>

<summary><h4 id="word-13-043" class="vocabulary-word-title">staircase<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-13-043" aria-label="定位 staircase">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="13-043" data-mark-label="staircase" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="staircase：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="staircase：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=staircase&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 staircase" title="在有道查询 staircase">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">13-043</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. (建筑内的)楼梯</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Each stair on the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=staircase&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>staircase</strong></a> leading to the upper storey needed another layer of paint.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">通向上层的楼梯上，每一级都需要再刷一层漆。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A layer of dust covered the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=staircase&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>staircase</strong></a>, making each stair difficult to see from the first storey.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">楼梯覆着一层灰，从一楼看不清每一级台阶。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The renovation added a protective layer to every stair and extended the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=staircase&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>staircase</strong></a> to the new storey.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">翻修为每级台阶加了保护层，并把楼梯延伸至新楼层。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a spiral staircase</span> · 螺旋楼梯</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=landing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">landing</a></span> · 楼梯平台</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tread&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tread</a></span> · 踏面</li></ul>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.【美】电梯</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=elevator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>elevator</strong></a> if you cannot hold the handrail safely on the escalator.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果无法安全握住扶梯扶手，就使用电梯。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The escalator stopped, so visitors followed the handrail towards the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=elevator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>elevator</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">扶梯停了，访客沿扶手走向电梯。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The new station provides an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=elevator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>elevator</strong></a> beside each escalator, with clear signs near the handrail.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新车站在每部扶梯旁设有电梯，扶手附近有清晰标识。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">take an elevator</span> · 乘电梯</p>

</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The new station provides an elevator beside each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=escalator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>escalator</strong></a>, with clear signs near the handrail.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新车站在每部扶梯旁设有电梯，扶手附近有清晰标识。</p></li>

### 词群 13.05｜建筑靠什么支撑和覆盖 {#group-13-05}

**串联方式：** 观察柱、梁、拱、穹顶与天花板，再看屋檐和烟囱。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-049">
<summary><h4 id="word-13-049" class="vocabulary-word-title">pillar<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-13-049" aria-label="定位 pillar">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="13-049" data-mark-label="pillar" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pillar：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pillar：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pillar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pillar" title="在有道查询 pillar">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">13-049</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.柱子；中坚，栋梁</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A stone <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pillar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pillar</strong></a> supports the arch, while a column and a timber beam reinforce the vault.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">石柱支撑着拱门，另一根柱子和一根木梁加固拱顶。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey recorded cracks in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pillar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pillar</strong></a>, column, beam, vault and entrance arch.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">勘察记录了支柱、圆柱、梁、拱顶及入口拱门的裂缝。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Restorers repaired the arch and vault before replacing the damaged beam above the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pillar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pillar</strong></a> and column.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">修复人员先修拱门和拱顶，再更换支柱与圆柱上方损坏的梁。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-050">
<summary><h4 id="word-13-050" class="vocabulary-word-title">column<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-13-050" aria-label="定位 column">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="13-050" data-mark-label="column" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="column：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="column：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=column&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 column" title="在有道查询 column">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">13-050</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.柱；(书、报纸等印刷页的)栏；(报刊的)专栏</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A stone pillar supports the arch, while a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=column&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>column</strong></a> and a timber beam reinforce the vault.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">石柱支撑着拱门，另一根柱子和一根木梁加固拱顶。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey recorded cracks in the pillar, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=column&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>column</strong></a>, beam, vault and entrance arch.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">勘察记录了支柱、圆柱、梁、拱顶及入口拱门的裂缝。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Restorers repaired the arch and vault before replacing the damaged beam above the pillar and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=column&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>column</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">修复人员先修拱门和拱顶，再更换支柱与圆柱上方损坏的梁。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a supporting column</span> · 支撑柱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=load&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">load</a></span> · 荷载</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=masonry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">masonry</a></span> · 砖石结构</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-051">
<summary><h4 id="word-13-051" class="vocabulary-word-title">beam<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-13-051" aria-label="定位 beam">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="13-051" data-mark-label="beam" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="beam：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="beam：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=beam&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 beam" title="在有道查询 beam">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">13-051</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. (能量、放射线或粒子的)束，柱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-usage"><span class="vocabulary-label">用法提示</span> 建筑语境中，beam 也指梁，如 a wooden beam（木梁）；与光束、射束的意思区分。</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A stone pillar supports the arch, while a column and a timber <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=beam&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>beam</strong></a> reinforce the vault.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">石柱支撑着拱门，另一根柱子和一根木梁加固拱顶。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey recorded cracks in the pillar, column, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=beam&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>beam</strong></a>, vault and entrance arch.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">勘察记录了支柱、圆柱、梁、拱顶及入口拱门的裂缝。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Restorers repaired the arch and vault before replacing the damaged <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=beam&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>beam</strong></a> above the pillar and column.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">修复人员先修拱门和拱顶，再更换支柱与圆柱上方损坏的梁。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a timber beam</span> · 木梁</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=load&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">load</a></span> · 荷载</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=masonry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">masonry</a></span> · 砖石结构</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-052">
<summary><h4 id="word-13-052" class="vocabulary-word-title">vault<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-13-052" aria-label="定位 vault">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="13-052" data-mark-label="vault" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="vault：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="vault：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vault&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 vault" title="在有道查询 vault">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">13-052</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.拱顶；金库，保险库</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A stone pillar supports the arch, while a column and a timber beam reinforce the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vault&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vault</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">石柱支撑着拱门，另一根柱子和一根木梁加固拱顶。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey recorded cracks in the pillar, column, beam, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vault&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vault</strong></a> and entrance arch.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">勘察记录了支柱、圆柱、梁、拱顶及入口拱门的裂缝。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Restorers repaired the arch and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vault&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vault</strong></a> before replacing the damaged beam above the pillar and column.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">修复人员先修拱门和拱顶，再更换支柱与圆柱上方损坏的梁。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a vaulted roof</span> · 拱顶屋顶</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=load&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">load</a></span> · 荷载</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=masonry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">masonry</a></span> · 砖石结构</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-053">
<summary><h4 id="word-13-053" class="vocabulary-word-title">arch<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-13-053" aria-label="定位 arch">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="13-053" data-mark-label="arch" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="arch：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="arch：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 arch" title="在有道查询 arch">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">13-053</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A stone pillar supports the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arch</strong></a>, while a column and a timber beam reinforce the vault.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">石柱支撑着拱门，另一根柱子和一根木梁加固拱顶。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey recorded cracks in the pillar, column, beam, vault and entrance <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arch</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">勘察记录了支柱、圆柱、梁、拱顶及入口拱门的裂缝。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Restorers repaired the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arch</strong></a> and vault before replacing the damaged beam above the pillar and column.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">修复人员先修拱门和拱顶，再更换支柱与圆柱上方损坏的梁。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a stone arch</span> · 石拱门</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=load&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">load</a></span> · 荷载</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=masonry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">masonry</a></span> · 砖石结构</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-054">
<summary><h4 id="word-13-054" class="vocabulary-word-title">ceiling<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-13-054" aria-label="定位 ceiling">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="13-054" data-mark-label="ceiling" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="ceiling：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="ceiling：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ceiling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 ceiling" title="在有道查询 ceiling">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">13-054</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.天花板，顶棚</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Smoke from the chimney drifted past the eaves while a stain spread across the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ceiling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ceiling</strong></a> inside.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">烟囱的烟飘过屋檐时，室内天花板上的污渍正在扩大。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The surveyor checked the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ceiling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ceiling</strong></a> below the chimney and the timber under the eaves.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">勘察员检查了烟囱下方的天花板和屋檐下的木材。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Repairing the chimney and eaves stopped water from damaging the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ceiling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ceiling</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">修好烟囱和屋檐后，水不再损坏天花板。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a high ceiling</span> · 高天花板</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=roofline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">roofline</a></span> · 屋顶轮廓</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gutter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">gutter</a></span> · 檐沟</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-055">
<summary><h4 id="word-13-055" class="vocabulary-word-title">eaves<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-13-055" aria-label="定位 eaves">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="13-055" data-mark-label="eaves" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="eaves：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="eaves：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eaves&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 eaves" title="在有道查询 eaves">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">13-055</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.屋檐</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Smoke from the chimney drifted past the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eaves&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>eaves</strong></a> while a stain spread across the ceiling inside.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">烟囱的烟飘过屋檐时，室内天花板上的污渍正在扩大。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The surveyor checked the ceiling below the chimney and the timber under the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eaves&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>eaves</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">勘察员检查了烟囱下方的天花板和屋檐下的木材。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Repairing the chimney and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eaves&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>eaves</strong></a> stopped water from damaging the ceiling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">修好烟囱和屋檐后，水不再损坏天花板。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">under the eaves</span> · 在屋檐下</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=roofline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">roofline</a></span> · 屋顶轮廓</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gutter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">gutter</a></span> · 檐沟</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-056">
<summary><h4 id="word-13-056" class="vocabulary-word-title">chimney<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-13-056" aria-label="定位 chimney">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="13-056" data-mark-label="chimney" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="chimney：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="chimney：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chimney&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 chimney" title="在有道查询 chimney">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">13-056</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.烟囱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Smoke from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chimney&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chimney</strong></a> drifted past the eaves while a stain spread across the ceiling inside.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">烟囱的烟飘过屋檐时，室内天花板上的污渍正在扩大。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The surveyor checked the ceiling below the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chimney&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chimney</strong></a> and the timber under the eaves.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">勘察员检查了烟囱下方的天花板和屋檐下的木材。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Repairing the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chimney&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chimney</strong></a> and eaves stopped water from damaging the ceiling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">修好烟囱和屋檐后，水不再损坏天花板。</p></li>

### 词群 13.06｜从大堂走进家庭空间 {#group-13-06}

**串联方式：** 先到接待处和大堂，再进入客厅、厨房与卫浴，辨认暖炉、阳台和家具。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-057">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The old <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=parlour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>parlour</strong></a> became a living room with a radiator, a restored fireplace and a cabinet for books.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旧客厅改成了起居室，配有暖气片、修复的壁炉和书柜。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=parlour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>parlour</strong></a>’s fireplace, the radiator in the living room needs no fuel cabinet nearby.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与旧客厅的壁炉不同，起居室的暖气片不需要旁边放燃料柜。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The cabinet moved from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=parlour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>parlour</strong></a> to the living room, where it stood between the fireplace and radiator.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">柜子从旧客厅搬到起居室，放在壁炉和暖气片之间。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.储藏柜；内阁</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The old parlour became a living room with a radiator, a restored fireplace and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cabinet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cabinet</strong></a> for books.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旧客厅改成了起居室，配有暖气片、修复的壁炉和书柜。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike the parlour’s fireplace, the radiator in the living room needs no fuel <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cabinet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cabinet</strong></a> nearby.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与旧客厅的壁炉不同，起居室的暖气片不需要旁边放燃料柜。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cabinet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cabinet</strong></a> moved from the parlour to the living room, where it stood between the fireplace and radiator.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">柜子从旧客厅搬到起居室，放在壁炉和暖气片之间。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a storage cabinet</span> · 储物柜</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=heating&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">heating</a></span> · 供暖</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=furnishing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">furnishing</a></span> · 室内陈设</li></ul>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An internal door links the kitchen to a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=balcony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>balcony</strong></a> overlooking the terrace.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一扇室内门把厨房与俯瞰露台的阳台相连。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The kitchen opens onto the terrace, while an internal staircase leads to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=balcony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>balcony</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">厨房通向露台，室内楼梯则通向阳台。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The plan removes an internal wall near the kitchen to improve access to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=balcony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>balcony</strong></a> and terrace.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">方案拆掉厨房附近的一面内墙，以方便前往阳台和露台。</p></li>
</ol>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

### 词群 13.08｜谁在工地上使用什么 {#group-13-08}

**串联方式：** 由木匠、石匠到砖瓦工程，再看脚手架、起重机和基础设施设备。

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=carpenter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>carpenter</strong></a> repaired the roof frame while the mason replaced a tile and restored the brickwork.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">木匠修屋架，泥瓦匠换了一块瓦并修复砖墙。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=carpenter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>carpenter</strong></a> and a mason inspected the damaged tile and the brickwork beneath it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">木匠和泥瓦匠检查了损坏的瓦片及其下方砖墙。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The mason checked the brickwork before asking the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=carpenter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>carpenter</strong></a> to strengthen the timber below the loose tile.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">泥瓦匠检查砖墙后，请木匠加固松动瓦片下面的木材。</p></li>
</ol>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The carpenter repaired the roof frame while the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mason&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mason</strong></a> replaced a tile and restored the brickwork.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">木匠修屋架，泥瓦匠换了一块瓦并修复砖墙。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A carpenter and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mason&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mason</strong></a> inspected the damaged tile and the brickwork beneath it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">木匠和泥瓦匠检查了损坏的瓦片及其下方砖墙。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mason&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mason</strong></a> checked the brickwork before asking the carpenter to strengthen the timber below the loose tile.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">泥瓦匠检查砖墙后，请木匠加固松动瓦片下面的木材。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a stone mason</span> · 石匠</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=craftsperson&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">craftsperson</a></span> · 工匠</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=roofing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">roofing</a></span> · 屋面施工</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-095">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.钩，钩状物；勾拳</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Pull the lever to move the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hook&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hook</strong></a> and lift the pole onto the scaffold.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">拉动操纵杆移动吊钩，把杆件吊上脚手架。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hook&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hook</strong></a> caught the pole while the operator held the lever beside the scaffold.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">操作员在脚手架旁握着操纵杆时，吊钩勾住了杆件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The inspection covered the scaffold pole, lifting <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hook&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hook</strong></a> and control lever.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">检查涵盖脚手架杆件、起吊钩和操纵杆。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a lifting hook</span> · 起吊钩</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A hook caught the pole while the operator held the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lever&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lever</strong></a> beside the scaffold.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">操作员在脚手架旁握着操纵杆时，吊钩勾住了杆件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The inspection covered the scaffold pole, lifting hook and control <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lever&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lever</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">检查涵盖脚手架杆件、起吊钩和操纵杆。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-099">
<summary><h4 id="word-13-099" class="vocabulary-word-title">infrastructure<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-13-099" aria-label="定位 infrastructure">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="13-099" data-mark-label="infrastructure" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="infrastructure：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="infrastructure：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=infrastructure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 infrastructure" title="在有道查询 infrastructure">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">13-099</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.基础设施</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A crane and other lifting apparatus are essential for this <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=infrastructure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>infrastructure</strong></a> project.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">起重机及其他起重装置对这项基础设施工程至关重要。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=infrastructure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>infrastructure</strong></a> team inspected the crane before using the apparatus near the bridge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">基础设施团队在桥边使用设备前检查了起重机。</p></li>

### 词群 13.09｜建筑设备出问题后怎样维修 {#group-13-09}

**串联方式：** 从故障进入维护和修理，再区分修改、修整、焊接、锯、拧、钻及管道作业。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-102">
<summary><h4 id="word-13-102" class="vocabulary-word-title">malfunction<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-13-102" aria-label="定位 malfunction">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="13-102" data-mark-label="malfunction" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="malfunction：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="malfunction：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=malfunction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 malfunction" title="在有道查询 malfunction">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">13-102</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.发生故障</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We maintain the machinery, fix any <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=malfunction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>malfunction</strong></a> and mend the protective fence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们维护机械、修复故障，并修补防护围栏。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The caretaker must maintain the boiler, fix it if a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=malfunction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>malfunction</strong></a> occurs and mend broken fittings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">管理员必须维护锅炉、出现故障时修理，并修补损坏配件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A technician can fix the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=malfunction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>malfunction</strong></a>, but staff must maintain the system and mend its damaged cover.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">技术员可以修复故障，但员工必须维护系统并修补损坏外罩。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a mechanical malfunction</span> · 机械故障</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=maintenance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">maintenance</a></span> · 维护</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repair</a></span> · 修理</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-103">
<summary><h4 id="word-13-103" class="vocabulary-word-title">maintain<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-13-103" aria-label="定位 maintain">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="13-103" data-mark-label="maintain" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="maintain：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="maintain：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=maintain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 maintain" title="在有道查询 maintain">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">13-103</span></span></summary>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=maintain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>maintain</strong></a> the machinery, fix any malfunction and mend the protective fence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们维护机械、修复故障，并修补防护围栏。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The caretaker must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=maintain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>maintain</strong></a> the boiler, fix it if a malfunction occurs and mend broken fittings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">管理员必须维护锅炉、出现故障时修理，并修补损坏配件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A technician can fix the malfunction, but staff must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=maintain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>maintain</strong></a> the system and mend its damaged cover.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">技术员可以修复故障，但员工必须维护系统并修补损坏外罩。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">maintain equipment</span> · 维护设备</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=maintenance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">maintenance</a></span> · 维护</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repair</a></span> · 修理</li></ul>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.稍作修改</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=modify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>modify</strong></a> the frame, trim the rough edges and weld the joints.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们将调整框架、修整粗糙边缘并焊接接头。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The fabricator had to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=modify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>modify</strong></a> the design before deciding where to trim and weld the steel.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">制作工必须先修改设计，再决定在哪里修切和焊接钢材。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You can trim the panel and weld it in place, but first <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=modify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>modify</strong></a> the support bracket.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">你可以修切面板再焊到位，但要先修改支架。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">modify a design</span> · 修改设计</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-109">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=saw&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>saw</strong></a> and drill to fit the cabinet, tighten each screw, then plumb in the sink.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用锯和钻安装橱柜，拧紧每颗螺丝，再接好水槽管道。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you plumb in the basin, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=saw&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>saw</strong></a> the panel to size, drill a hole and insert the screw.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">接洗手盆管道前，先把板锯成合适大小，钻孔，再拧入螺丝。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The installer packed a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=saw&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>saw</strong></a>, a drill and a screw kit before arriving to plumb in the dishwasher.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">安装工带好锯、钻和螺丝套装，前来接洗碗机管道。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a hand saw</span> · 手锯</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plumber&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">plumber</a></span> · 水管工</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=toolbox&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">toolbox</a></span> · 工具箱</li></ul>

</details>

### 词群 13.10｜基础工程与室内配置 {#group-13-10}

**串联方式：** 由高架桥、跨度和大坝进入装配、安装、配家具，再设计摆放与布局。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-113">
<summary><h4 id="word-13-113" class="vocabulary-word-title">viaduct<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-13-113" aria-label="定位 viaduct">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="13-113" data-mark-label="viaduct" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="viaduct：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="viaduct：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=viaduct&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 viaduct" title="在有道查询 viaduct">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">13-113</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.高架桥</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=viaduct&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>viaduct</strong></a>’s longest span crosses the river below the dam.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">高架桥最长的跨度跨过大坝下游的河流。</p></li>

### 词群 13.11｜进出、内外与朝向 {#group-13-11}

**串联方式：** 从入口、进入和出口组织移动，再对照内外位置、表面与方向。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="13-122">
<summary><h4 id="word-13-122" class="vocabulary-word-title">entrance<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-13-122" aria-label="定位 entrance">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="13-122" data-mark-label="entrance" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="entrance：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="entrance：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=entrance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 entrance" title="在有道查询 entrance">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">13-122</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.入口；进入权</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Entry is free, but enter through the main <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=entrance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>entrance</strong></a> and leave by the marked exit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">入场免费，但请从主入口进入，从标记出口离开。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Visitors seeking entry must enter at the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=entrance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>entrance</strong></a> beside the emergency exit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">需要入场的访客须从紧急出口旁的入口进入。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=entrance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>entrance</strong></a> controls entry, while a separate exit lets people leave without blocking those who enter.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">入口管控入场，独立出口让离场者不会阻挡进入的人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a main entrance</span> · 主入口</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=access&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">access</a></span> · 进入通道</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evacuation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">evacuation</a></span> · 疏散</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 本章 Language Takeaway

优先尝试主动说出的三个表达：

- **public infrastructure**：公共基础设施；public 为补充词。
- **a spacious apartment**：宽敞的公寓。
- **maintain the building**：维护建筑。

### 串学示范｜自拟短文

A well-designed building needs a stable structure and a practical layout. The entrance should lead clearly to the lobby, stairs or elevator. A spacious living room and an accessible kitchen can improve daily life. Regular maintenance helps keep the building and its equipment in good condition.

**中文理解：** 设计良好的建筑需要稳定的结构和实用的布局。入口应能清楚通向大堂、楼梯或电梯。宽敞的客厅和便于使用的厨房可以改善日常生活。定期维护有助于保持建筑与设备的良好状态。

### 闭卷回忆｜先尝试，再逐级提示

**任务：** 带人参观一栋建筑：位置、类型、结构、通行、房间和维护。

1. 关掉本章正文，在空白页列出你记得的词群；每群先写 3—5 个英文词或搭配。
2. 从中选 3 个词群，连成 3—5 句英文；用明确关系连接，专业分类和事实不确定时先核对。
3. 卡住时，先只看“本章主线”；还想不起，再看词群标题和中文简释，最后才看英文。
4. 用核对编号找出遗漏词；把“见到认得”与“想表达时能说出”分开记录。
5. 合上提示重新说一遍；下次换一个对象、地点或例子，检查能否迁移。

可直接复制的空白页：

```text
主题：建筑场所

看提示后才想起的核对编号：
下次换什么情境再说：
```
